##### What was that? TikTok enters the podcast business, Google’s Chat & Shop AI, GDPR reform, Reddit interactive ads, LinkedIn’s AI-powered job search, and everything else that matters.

This newsletter is supported by our friends at [Tracksuit](https://substack.com/redirect/7da9d041-421a-4c15-8036-dad6ad23acdd?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI_zv_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g), the affordable, always-on brand tracking dashboard. 

> Before you go further, Attention is like money–you can waste it or invest it. If you invest it in my paid newsletter, you'll get dividends for years.

[![](https://substackcdn.com/image/fetch/$s_!VaNH!,w_1100,c_limit,f_auto,q_auto:good,fl_progressive:steep/https%3A%2F%2Fsubstack-post-media.s3.amazonaws.com%2Fpublic%2Fimages%2Fe2e1fbaf-720d-4dbf-a79e-fea6ad235a33_1100x220.png)](https://substack.com/redirect/1a809d7e-73d5-49dd-bd72-2e0a26f4b8d2?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI_zv_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g)* TikTok [launches](https://substack.com/redirect/bc404ab1-232c-4d04-9afb-c046787689e1?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI_zv_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g) podcast network [with](https://substack.com/redirect/8cae30f5-ea4a-47fe-8c7b-f251e6575147?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI_zv_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g) iHeartMedia.
* [Google](https://substack.com/redirect/00d0756a-8c3f-47fc-87d4-556f0b8934d2?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI_zv_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g) is rolling out [conversational shopping](https://substack.com/redirect/8af80ff1-9556-4150-bf3a-8310f6c253f9?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI_zv_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g)—and [ads](https://substack.com/redirect/d84af09a-9dee-464b-b09c-91af858e786c?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI_zv_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g)—in AI Mode search.
* Disney and YouTube TV have a deal: ESPN and ABC [return to streaming service](https://substack.com/redirect/fa411ef7-f0d5-4f03-81e0-f6c899b90b5a?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI_zv_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g) after two week blackout.
* EU Commission [about to](https://substack.com/redirect/d05b32c2-4254-4fcc-8f0a-06ed35d85ae7?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI_zv_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g) [wreck](https://substack.com/redirect/d52376b0-230b-4ea3-826e-77bab2b03011?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI_zv_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g) core principles of the [GDPR](https://substack.com/redirect/81842a66-72d9-4cec-9ef3-016538b564ad?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI_zv_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g).
* Reddit unveils [interactive ad format](https://substack.com/redirect/d932f9e2-c6a0-485b-aa0d-58f7170a3aa9?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI_zv_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g), piloted by [Paramount](https://substack.com/redirect/de275fa1-da4a-431a-92b9-b0c73d850697?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI_zv_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g).
* [LinkedIn](https://substack.com/redirect/1f9a9455-058f-414c-9dbd-fb312ed50c3b?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI_zv_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g) is making it easier to [search for people with AI](https://substack.com/redirect/33834b84-563c-4424-a9c7-e5bc45de86c8?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI_zv_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g).

---

[![](https://substackcdn.com/image/fetch/$s_!zM4F!,w_1100,c_limit,f_auto,q_auto:good,fl_progressive:steep/https%3A%2F%2Fsubstack-post-media.s3.amazonaws.com%2Fpublic%2Fimages%2F484d6c9a-ab2b-4a12-b322-17f701d13b66_1100x220.png)](https://substack.com/redirect/f7eb20a6-e7e8-4300-a972-6846526caf8c?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI_zv_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g)* [Vine is back](https://substack.com/redirect/2fc148f7-e7b9-4575-b4d1-6b05b4041892?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI_zv_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g), sort of: New app [shuts out AI](https://substack.com/redirect/93b579c8-a402-44c5-ad1c-571af186c988?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI_zv_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g) as thousands of old videos return.
* Sky Sports axes woman-focused [TikTok channel Halo](https://substack.com/redirect/54268993-fd2c-4b24-8acc-c09cd0e17483?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI_zv_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g) following fierce backlash.
	+ This was a disaster and possibly one of the worst TikTok marketing decisions ever.
* [Former CEO of WPP](https://substack.com/redirect/3e886a14-f5cb-404e-a45b-56875a5a4279?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI_zv_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g) that produces ‘Love Island’ sues [ad agency](https://substack.com/redirect/4d330c43-7208-4d49-88a9-c6d13add1903?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI_zv_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g) for $100 million.
	+ WPP draws [interest](https://substack.com/redirect/5bfd170c-03cb-4690-9df0-1792bd75de4f?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI_zv_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g) from Havas and private equity, Times says.
		- WPP brings in [McKinsey](https://substack.com/redirect/b08bfb74-b270-4989-b3a5-af5bb400314c?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI_zv_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g) to advise on strategic review.
* The age of [anti-social media](https://substack.com/redirect/a005e863-26fe-4877-b627-2954748f6060?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI_zv_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g) is here
* Snapchat is [bringing back](https://substack.com/redirect/a38c40f4-269d-47fd-9a44-13ac57af875b?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI_zv_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g) classic 2D Bitmoji.
* EU launches antitrust probe into how [Google’s anti-spam policy](https://substack.com/redirect/280fc4c9-a885-4f28-bdb4-4b94d32f55bb?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI_zv_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g) affects publishers’ search rankings.
* [AI slop hits new high](https://substack.com/redirect/bb9948c0-e6c6-443c-839d-d6b4fb9365c6?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI_zv_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g) as fake country artist goes to #1 on Billboard digital songs chart.
* Paramount+ is growing up, and executives have decided [it’s time to retire its free trial](https://substack.com/redirect/eb182acf-bf46-4b34-84a1-b7d976978bab?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI_zv_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g).

---

> *In partnership with [Tracksuit](https://substack.com/redirect/21419bca-21df-4c5c-91bd-a10105f21abf?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI_zv_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g)* 

> Marketers are under the pump to go big or go home. They need to perform, entertain, grow *and* [prove](https://substack.com/redirect/21419bca-21df-4c5c-91bd-a10105f21abf?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI_zv_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g) the ROI of brand spend.

> If you’re trying to take a big bet instead of creating the same cookie cutter campaigns each quarter, you need evidence it improved awareness or shifted perception in your key audience segments — not just reached a million people.

> [Tracksuit’s](https://substack.com/redirect/21419bca-21df-4c5c-91bd-a10105f21abf?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI_zv_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g) always-on brand tracking that helps you prove the impact of your boldest campaign work over time…so you’ll [get the budget](https://substack.com/redirect/21419bca-21df-4c5c-91bd-a10105f21abf?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI_zv_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g) for the next moderately chaotic idea.

---

* Amid search wars, [Google touts YouTube](https://substack.com/redirect/b45ed81b-644b-4d29-a9aa-243646082de5?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI_zv_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g), display inventory to advertisers.
* Brands tap [outside-the-box personalities](https://substack.com/redirect/c2db290e-bd1a-4402-852d-a2166f525f11?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI_zv_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g) to stand out as influencer marketing gets more competitive.
* Amazon Prime Video [ad-supported reach hits 315M](https://substack.com/redirect/15fc0794-9c6c-4bed-ae07-324ac9acbc72?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI_zv_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g) monthly viewers.
* Michael Caine and Matthew McConaughey partner with [ElevenLabs](https://substack.com/redirect/f64569c9-cb0d-4683-90cb-35bc593c01d8?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI_zv_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g) for AI voice cloning.
* The [fight to save](https://substack.com/redirect/f3ee2b08-bc60-463e-a77d-f4fcb3bf6b36?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI_zv_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g) the $2.7 billion botox empire.
* In Trump 2.0, MAGA-aligned influencers and media emerge as [the new mainstream](https://substack.com/redirect/a957af20-f5fb-49fa-bcfe-dc03779d794f?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI_zv_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g).
	+ Influencers are [royalty](https://substack.com/redirect/3dce8d4b-4442-401a-abfc-0fd1ae9d5192?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI_zv_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g) at this college, and the turf war is vicious.
* ‘[Beast Land](https://substack.com/redirect/e9081fe9-c757-4b1d-8b74-cbc42122fb89?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI_zv_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g)’ opens in Saudi Arabia: Everything [we know](https://substack.com/redirect/360dced7-b5ae-4811-90ce-6b89a9bdd3e1?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI_zv_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g) about MrBeast’s new theme park.
* Who pays when [A.I. is wrong](https://substack.com/redirect/f8d3c0fe-7fb0-474d-bd85-3d2545fe422c?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI_zv_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g)?
* Arthur Sadoun urges Omnicom to [report financials](https://substack.com/redirect/8832015c-17f7-4d82-a1f5-dbb100d2c2a1?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI_zv_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g) like the other big four holdcos.
* [Steam Machine](https://substack.com/redirect/bd045a9d-78a8-4792-b084-3c9c8714a113?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI_zv_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g): Valve [rivals](https://substack.com/redirect/c0622505-553f-4ec3-a1f9-94710e8432e5?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI_zv_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g) Xbox and PlayStation with new console.

The company is [exploring AI](https://substack.com/redirect/ea24bf31-e9b1-4061-ac96-84ab740f95b5?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI_zv_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g) so fans can make AI slop content from Disney stories.
	+ Disney to [increase content spending](https://substack.com/redirect/d34644ff-a96b-41f4-8a60-096e7b94343d?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI_zv_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g) by $1 billion next year.
* EU: Chat Control [isn’t](https://substack.com/redirect/08e42046-437f-4d3e-ab42-c6dd87e6400c?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI_zv_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g) dead, [Denmark](https://substack.com/redirect/36e8534f-22bb-4971-a435-abd14f794a47?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI_zv_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g) has a new proposal.
* Under Armour splits with [Steph Curry](https://substack.com/redirect/c98f5d04-b4eb-4315-aa18-824f5c7205e6?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI_zv_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g), leaving NBA legend free to find a new business partner.
* The viral TikToks [testing](https://substack.com/redirect/6e0944fa-4f25-45f0-b475-4c22d217f52d?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI_zv_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g) churches.
* J.P. Morgan calls out [AI spend](https://substack.com/redirect/8f39b219-afee-4724-8248-e93454870571?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI_zv_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g), says $650 billion in [annual revenue required](https://substack.com/redirect/7ca3a7ec-a4cf-4bd8-ac88-92170f12552d?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI_zv_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g) to deliver mere 10% return on AI buildout.

[Sonder](https://substack.com/redirect/1f3a7103-0b2d-459b-8840-b8a926a8a6a1?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI_zv_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g) files for bankruptcy [after](https://substack.com/redirect/8a56ca71-aff0-4423-bd04-db4a06f5d923?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI_zv_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g) its Marriott breakup.
* Netflix wants to build a home for its fans — [at the mall](https://substack.com/redirect/1d819484-4cd0-4c81-aad3-a232e4d4bd27?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI_zv_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g).

* Gen Z’s hottest new job: [Nannying](https://substack.com/redirect/11abfcbe-174c-43df-9976-c7e56832328a?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI_zv_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g) for billionaires.
* IPG [sheds](https://substack.com/redirect/fc5370f9-4221-4148-9170-12f7e76ee8f9?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI_zv_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g) [3,200 jobs](https://substack.com/redirect/fc5370f9-4221-4148-9170-12f7e76ee8f9?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI_zv_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g), vacates office space ahead of Omnicom merger.

> [Tracksuit](https://substack.com/redirect/21419bca-21df-4c5c-91bd-a10105f21abf?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI_zv_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g) and Small World’s [‘Entertain or Die’ report](https://substack.com/redirect/c5c6cda1-d0b2-4412-9fb3-09d8ceebed6c?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI_zv_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g) reveals how brands are building in-house Hollywoods, worlds consumers escape into, filled with brand characters, lore, and social content that keeps them talking.\*\*

> 😶‍🌫️ Report Break: While 70% of adults surveyed in the U.K. worry about economy-wide AI impacts, 39% worry about AI-related consequences for their own occupation. In the U.S., 72% of surveyed adults said they worried about the [broader economic effects of AI](https://substack.com/redirect/ecd86804-2de3-4170-9d94-bf92e3f9d462?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI_zv_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g), while 47% said they were concerned about their own jobs.

* Google ordered to pay [$665 million](https://substack.com/redirect/8e64e8ef-92d9-4c1c-921c-6cd42aa0a197?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI_zv_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g) for anticompetitive practices in Germany.
	+ Google accused in suit of using [Gemini AI tool](https://substack.com/redirect/bdc98318-460a-4d9c-b1f4-361adac367df?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI_zv_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g) to snoop on users.
* [Breaking down](https://substack.com/redirect/aa694e67-7843-4eef-98b2-6aae00a3c99b?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI_zv_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g) the EU’s investigation into Google’s Search policy.
	+ Google defends [Parasite SEO](https://substack.com/redirect/5df1089a-431f-42e6-b721-34f4c1f4b644?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI_zv_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g) crackdown.
* Google takes on Pinterest with a dedicated ‘[inspirational](https://substack.com/redirect/0c6afdbb-08bb-440e-af74-fb6ebb608aed?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI_zv_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g)’ images tab in its mobile app.
* Gemini in Google Drive can now make [Audio Overviews](https://substack.com/redirect/d1c452bd-c120-40f0-a407-3f34b1e22fc5?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI_zv_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g) of PDFs.
* Google Ads experiment taps [social creatives](https://substack.com/redirect/251c1dc7-e143-4999-87b7-1344a6deec65?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI_zv_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g) for PMax campaigns.
* Google has chosen a side in [Trump’s mass deportation effort](https://substack.com/redirect/03a2025f-45fd-48e3-a510-bfc9d2215fb8?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI_zv_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g).
* Google promises [Discover ‘fix’ as more fake AI stories](https://substack.com/redirect/d0a08ccd-e17e-4246-91d3-b2e7062d7e16?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI_zv_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g) top rankings.
* Google quietly adds [brand inclusions](https://substack.com/redirect/0e3b4c1d-d8ac-45c5-8af2-5f70610a9b15?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI_zv_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g) to standard Shopping campaigns.

---

* Google [releases](https://substack.com/redirect/4072ff95-5c30-4ebf-8b16-a267a448b88c?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI_zv_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g) comprehensive guide to responsive search ads optimization.

* Google Ads Editor 2.11 gets [campaign-level negatives](https://substack.com/redirect/85417c4a-5a90-4ed4-8516-e849e7ce4856?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI_zv_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g) and smarter automation.
* [Google Photos](https://substack.com/redirect/e09d2f11-4c06-45c0-8ae8-15c7abebe957?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI_zv_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g) adds new AI features for editing, expands AI-powered search to over 100 countries.
* Google Ads boosts accuracy in [advertiser account suspensions](https://substack.com/redirect/7fe6ecca-112d-4369-93f6-2e697e7a1fd9?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI_zv_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g).
* Google [snubs](https://substack.com/redirect/ceede53e-5e4e-44a6-90b2-3bd6f924e289?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI_zv_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g) EU request to break itself up.
	+ Google offers [EU to change adtech policy](https://substack.com/redirect/6245ddde-8366-442a-a866-00efe5bd39ca?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI_zv_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g), no divestment.
* [South Korea](https://substack.com/redirect/5492f00c-029a-4a3d-84fc-d57b1a13a6b6?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI_zv_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g) delays decision on Google’s request to export map data.
* Google plans [$40 billion Texas data center](https://substack.com/redirect/d118003d-1aed-48c9-86f3-8d4a7177145c?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI_zv_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g) investment amid AI boom.
	+ Google is still aiming for its “[moonshot](https://substack.com/redirect/f13b52a1-bfc8-4a51-a9ba-e4f646bc2857?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI_zv_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g)” 2030 energy goals.
* Google [launches](https://substack.com/redirect/cea41474-8696-4287-84aa-d48942d05314?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI_zv_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g) a lawsuit targeting text message scammers.
* Google [launches](https://substack.com/redirect/3d2fe407-97c7-4872-834c-cf11fe4c56a4?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI_zv_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g) Gemini-powered AI Agents for advertising and analytics.

[![](https://substackcdn.com/image/fetch/$s_!J7DY!,w_1100,c_limit,f_auto,q_auto:good,fl_progressive:steep/https%3A%2F%2Fsubstack-post-media.s3.amazonaws.com%2Fpublic%2Fimages%2Facd06a12-7508-4bfa-91b1-8c434a20fc84_1100x220.png)](https://substack.com/redirect/ce6d0787-aeef-4c0f-876d-fc50ae97f4c2?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI_zv_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g)* X will show a warning on user accounts if [someone tries to use a VPN](https://substack.com/redirect/215bcf54-463f-418f-8c0b-6ff3eb026e4f?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI_zv_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g) to hide their account location when the upcoming ‘About Your Account’ feature launches.
* Irish media regulator opens [investigation](https://substack.com/redirect/4ddc4805-5b7a-4959-87f8-733b6def4336?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI_zv_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g) into [Musk’s X](https://substack.com/redirect/e14bb300-eae8-4fab-94eb-4b33632e1f18?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI_zv_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g).
* X rolls out its Signal-like [encrypted chat](https://substack.com/redirect/f36bde99-f6b4-4a1e-88c7-5fb8dfd7168a?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI_zv_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g) feature.
	+ Users [criticize](https://substack.com/redirect/32e818b9-063e-423b-b580-6526a431bb70?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI_zv_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g) new encrypted chat feature that replaces direct messages.
* Elon Musk says report on xAI’s $15 billion fundraise is ‘[false](https://substack.com/redirect/f9f85faa-ca38-401a-a0a4-e2cdc36100e0?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI_zv_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g)’.
* X will soon let you [sort your following feed](https://substack.com/redirect/fd0847b8-195e-4a7d-ab28-1caf43bf93f4?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI_zv_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g) by “popular”.
* Elon Musk’s Grok AI briefly says [Trump won 2020](https://substack.com/redirect/59783f6c-c030-4205-8891-41d513e6686c?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI_zv_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g) presidential election.
* X adds [new parameters](https://substack.com/redirect/17d53da2-5c12-4096-bd63-9a3212dc7843?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI_zv_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g) for buying handles.
* xAI delays [Grok 5 release](https://substack.com/redirect/197f6478-d271-4a25-ac44-1cb2d9ee004e?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI_zv_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g) to next year.
* Apple and OpenAI [fail](https://substack.com/redirect/2e57105e-7277-4881-9a4d-9600e8c8fccc?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI_zv_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g) to dismiss [Elon Musk’s antitrust lawsuit](https://substack.com/redirect/a07119c7-efa0-4f78-bf39-82c1c041a662?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI_zv_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g).
* X appears to be [sending fake traffic](https://substack.com/redirect/cd968ddf-71da-4f38-a456-65d58bd04da4?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI_zv_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g) across the web.
* Spain fines X [$5.8 million](https://substack.com/redirect/ea3a25e4-3538-454e-8ceb-70cf6442e2f7?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI_zv_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g) in cryptoasset advertising case.
* X [awards](https://substack.com/redirect/88175141-391d-4e16-9cc5-ddf24c723967?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI_zv_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g) first set of ‘Certified Banger’ posts.
* xAI works on [Grok Code Remote](https://substack.com/redirect/94ffbdbb-0d13-416b-ba53-85b484474308?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI_zv_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g) to rival OpenAI’s Codex.

> 🥀 Report Break: [Insiders promoted to the CEO role](https://substack.com/redirect/59e33f48-b7e1-410a-9493-a85afc3c480a?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI_zv_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g) consistently outperform outside hires, according to a new study by the Yale School of Management. “It’s not even close,” said Professor Jeffrey Sonnenfeld, who conducted the research into the past five years’ Fortune 500 appointments with his colleague Steven Tian.

[![](https://substackcdn.com/image/fetch/$s_!85wj!,w_1100,c_limit,f_auto,q_auto:good,fl_progressive:steep/https%3A%2F%2Fsubstack-post-media.s3.amazonaws.com%2Fpublic%2Fimages%2F20a7b272-bbeb-4fe3-a619-d76e41822174_1100x220.png)](https://substack.com/redirect/faa56247-32a5-433c-b681-9cc854dc2f8a?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI_zv_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g)* Snapchat’s [new report](https://substack.com/redirect/769b7140-2822-4a25-b4ea-3383fc0ebb8b?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI_zv_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g) shares insights into the online creator experience. 
	+ Long-term partnerships that allow for more meaningful connections and stronger conversions are important to creators. In fact, 45% of creators with over 100,000 followers are looking for this type of partnership.
		- Additionally, brands can reach out to creators in a variety of ways, as 54% of creators say they’re typically contacted via social platforms, 45% say direct message through apps, and 44% say via email.
	+ Snapchat published [another report](https://substack.com/redirect/c64b771a-6ed1-48f6-8b61-7e732fc3100e?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI_zv_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g) on grocery shopping trends.
	+ Disney and Snapchat [launch](https://substack.com/redirect/bd13d47f-0d41-4469-bcf5-aeedac8fc63f?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI_zv_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g) marketing campaign for Zootopia 2.
* How [Gen Z made Pinterest](https://substack.com/redirect/c09aed78-768a-4eed-b3d0-70fc36124c0f?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI_zv_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g) feel new again.
* Patreon is adding [a discovery feed](https://substack.com/redirect/f3394b22-3308-4e85-8252-f6ce54dc5c4f?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI_zv_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g) to help creators avoid social media platforms.
* Reddit puts [more focus](https://substack.com/redirect/54a293d1-4c24-4939-b072-7b06b01b06a5?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI_zv_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g) on automated targeting in ads manager.
	+ ​Reddit welcomes [Maria Angelidou-Smith](https://substack.com/redirect/456ebf54-232e-4bd2-aa41-e1969f3ddb93?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI_zv_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g) as Chief Product Officer.
	+ Reddit’s cofounder had [a bad feeling](https://substack.com/redirect/c333a1b9-b90a-4a02-8f0b-adf93de5a359?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI_zv_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g) about giving data to ‘cunning’ Sam Altman.
	+ Women are discussing their [next car purchase](https://substack.com/redirect/6fe6ce80-bb28-4c48-a12f-549f250d7e6d?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI_zv_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g) on Reddit.
	+ [Reddit for WooCommerce](https://substack.com/redirect/c38e5a71-8c7e-4eee-abe8-924df4558640?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI_zv_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g) integration is now generally available.
	+ [Reddit mod jailed](https://substack.com/redirect/0d720c99-e973-4393-b31a-2983de8777c0?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI_zv_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g) for sharing movie sex scenes in rare “moral rights” verdict.
* Apple TV abandons [extra paywall](https://substack.com/redirect/f99e20a7-9496-44d8-b043-5dbfef7c4135?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI_zv_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g) for Major League Soccer games.
	+ Apple [introduces](https://substack.com/redirect/44aac018-f177-437c-bd28-f7fc0d2b2bfe?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI_zv_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g) Digital ID, a new way to create and present [an ID](https://substack.com/redirect/3d964392-8d8c-4fe1-be2c-a7c03f543e64?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI_zv_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g) in Apple Wallet.
	+ Apple’s new [App Review Guidelines](https://substack.com/redirect/833e052f-bbe0-42dc-9701-eb0df6baa514?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI_zv_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g) clamp down on apps sharing personal data with ‘third-party AI’.
* Spotify tests [AI-powered audiobook recaps](https://substack.com/redirect/962a5694-6b80-4c6f-9bcb-2e6c1dae6514?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI_zv_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g) on iOS, expands playlist Shuffle controls.

* Brave: The Little-Known Search and Browser Startup generating [$100 million](https://substack.com/redirect/d5c1d7bd-0e9c-4496-9452-cee7171adeef?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI_zv_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g) in annualized revenue.
* Can [Beehiiv](https://substack.com/redirect/b054d9a4-27bb-4391-8ecd-63ab31b8268c?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI_zv_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g) carve out a bigger slice of the creator economy?
	+ Beehiiv adds AI website building, creator tools in [major expansion](https://substack.com/redirect/e6e7cc40-f183-43fc-9c60-0b3b31a92a60?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI_zv_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g).
* Visa unveils 2025 creator report; introduces [Creator Agent Pilot](https://substack.com/redirect/f119b2f3-fca5-4b93-b3ff-99c4b1769a3e?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI_zv_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g) with Karat.
	+ Creators surveyed are global entrepreneurs, with 52% receiving payments from outside of their home country

	+ [Wikipedia founder](https://substack.com/redirect/422d1677-bd1c-4cde-8bb1-211ea27419ed?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI_zv_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g) says ‘Gaza genocide’ page lacked neutrality.
	+ Wikipedia and the Destruction of Trust. ([video](https://substack.com/redirect/444a121b-7b22-44be-8235-ca4adae02d9b?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI_zv_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g))
* TravelCreatorsX to launch [first creator summit](https://substack.com/redirect/2bf37425-2b00-481e-ac40-71f6dea6a115?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI_zv_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g) inside U.S. Travel Adventure Shows
* Cigna launches mental health collaboration with [Headspace](https://substack.com/redirect/b652188d-a739-4d48-8802-6086a291f4a2?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI_zv_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g).
* [Uber](https://substack.com/redirect/28a82242-b3d9-4282-80f1-15d60b770adb?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI_zv_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g) is making it easier to get a ride at the airport or pay for someone else’s.
* Amazon Fire TV will now [block apps used for piracy](https://substack.com/redirect/ee31dfb7-70d6-48a7-a504-ecb980c811a9?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI_zv_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g), even if you sideloaded them.
	+ Brands can now buy [interactive Amazon Prime ads](https://substack.com/redirect/2266de3a-6926-4474-a1d9-31dec78f74c0?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI_zv_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g) targeting specific locations.
	+ Amazon Music adds ‘[Fan Groups](https://substack.com/redirect/3b2a8661-d844-4531-9c07-97286a330b69?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI_zv_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g)’ to allow listeners to discover, share and chat in dedicated communities.
* Netflix wants [50+ shows as part of its big podcast push](https://substack.com/redirect/46c5d0d5-8a86-45c8-a148-ac7cef676e62?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI_zv_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g), but winning creators from YouTube won’t be easy.
* [Figma](https://substack.com/redirect/50933de4-55a9-4893-9498-a834d2b2bb82?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI_zv_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g) bets on India to expand beyond design.
* People are [dumping](https://substack.com/redirect/d6197647-6534-41e1-9bec-4cfb56f06453?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI_zv_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g) Tinder. The dating app wants to reignite its spark by getting singles offline.
* Disney stock plunges as [Q4 revenue misses](https://substack.com/redirect/61f73db9-cf1a-4c56-977d-beedb4f9ce20?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI_zv_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g) estimates amid linear TV pressures.

> Report Break: 89% of creators claim to [lack access to specialized mental health resources](https://substack.com/redirect/f7c0631e-e033-409d-a531-4e6929ee580b?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI_zv_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g).

> 58% of respondents said their self-worth declines when their content underperforms, and 69% reported financial insecurity as a result of their work. That status has been associated with [deleterious mental health outcomes](https://substack.com/redirect/f3f33ffa-39fc-4add-84c3-6ecd1974b3b0?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI_zv_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g).

* Why [statistics aren’t reflecting the economy](https://substack.com/redirect/2ee89fbd-1f18-445a-bffc-4c7dec20eff3?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI_zv_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g) and everyday American life.
* Salesforce buys [AI startup](https://substack.com/redirect/b1671440-0804-4e55-b2c5-8c2b0d1c9a84?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI_zv_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g) to boost its enterprise search abilities.
* Act Climate Labs [launches](https://substack.com/redirect/ac5921a2-43d7-42f5-ad17-79ce0faecb33?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI_zv_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g) blueprint to phase out fossil fuel advertising.
* [Supermetrics AI](https://substack.com/redirect/603363c1-7525-4130-aeda-9d55b17be126?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI_zv_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g) launches, reinforced by 15 years of marketing intelligence experience.
* IAB Tech Lab [unveils](https://substack.com/redirect/10e3f50a-8b3e-4def-8571-4991b883a6df?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI_zv_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g) Agentic RTB Framework to boost real-time ad trading efficiency.
* AB Tasty [launches](https://substack.com/redirect/5154b398-8d73-4e2e-82f2-ee28cd7145e1?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI_zv_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g) Evi, a new evidence-based marketing agent.
* MFA increased by 103% while ad clutter sites saw a 38% rise during Diwali, across India and Singapore: [IAS Report](https://substack.com/redirect/a919b6df-8cb7-4fcd-a3e6-5626527867a3?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI_zv_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g).
* [Nextdoor](https://substack.com/redirect/6eee0c90-33b6-481a-bbd1-12aef3947a0e?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI_zv_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g) gives its local ad platform an AI-powered upgrade.
* Prain Global [debuts](https://substack.com/redirect/b5fa317d-4ca5-418a-846e-42dd553f25c0?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI_zv_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g) global PR alliance ‘PGA Plus.’
* Omnisend and [Triple Whale](https://substack.com/redirect/20d55c9b-21e9-4601-8487-230fb8f6171c?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI_zv_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g) partner to give e-commerce brands a unified view of marketing performance.
* 24 Seven [acquires](https://substack.com/redirect/0f88d107-3b9c-4b06-9fb6-d0c7f729caf7?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI_zv_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g) Marketing Consulting Firm Markacy.
* [Anonymised](https://substack.com/redirect/342472b2-de19-463e-84a5-2b46967342c9?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI_zv_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g) & Bytek partnership enables secure first-party data activation for expanded customer reach.
* Kevel and [Skai](https://substack.com/redirect/c8ad257f-280b-42f4-8686-897a9cbf76f7?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI_zv_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g) announce new partnership to expand retail media access.
* [Stagwell](https://substack.com/redirect/0ef8e4bc-f8d8-4452-a0d7-d8ce3cd8f799?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI_zv_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g) launches new bipartisan public affairs firm.
* Hightouch [launches](https://substack.com/redirect/f39da4b3-957b-40f4-a3ec-be4d04f44ed9?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI_zv_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g) high-performance agents to help marketers.

> 🧶 Report Break: According to The Conference Board Holiday Spending Survey, the average US consumer intends to [spend](https://substack.com/redirect/581fe849-f165-4993-8c17-e15702b65647?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI_zv_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g) [$990 on holiday-related purchases](https://substack.com/redirect/581fe849-f165-4993-8c17-e15702b65647?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI_zv_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g) in 2025. That’s down 6.9% from $1063 in 2024 and close to holiday spending intentions in 2023 ($985), but lower than in 2022 ($1,006) and 2021 ($1,022).

<div dir="auto" style="--image-offset-margin: -120px;padding: 32px 0 0 0;font-size: 16px;line-height: 26px;"><div dir="auto" style="text-align: initial;font-size: 16px;line-height: 26px;width: 100%;word-break: break-word;margin-bottom: 16px;font-family: Spectral,sans-serif;font-weight: 400;">
<p style="margin: 0 0 20px 0;color: rgb(54,55,55);line-height: 26px;font-size: 16px;margin-top: 0;"><span>This newsletter is supported by our friends at </span><a href="https://substack.com/redirect/7da9d041-421a-4c15-8036-dad6ad23acdd?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI\_zv\_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g" rel="" style="color: #e85c57;text-decoration: none;">Tracksuit</a><span>, the affordable, always-on brand tracking dashboard. </span></p>
<blockquote style="border-left: 4px solid #e85c57;margin: 20px 0;padding: 0;"><p style="margin: 0 0 20px 0;color: rgb(54,55,55);margin-left: 20px;line-height: 26px;font-size: 16px;">Before you go further, Attention is like money–you can waste it or invest it. If you invest it in my paid newsletter, you'll get dividends for years.</p></blockquote>
<p data-attrs='{"url":"https://thesocialjuice.substack.com/bec55781","text":"The Social Juice+ (more of what matters)","action":null,"class":"button-wrapper"}' data-component-name="ButtonCreateButton" style="margin: 0 0 20px 0;color: rgb(54,55,55);line-height: 26px;font-size: 16px;text-align: center;cursor: pointer;border-radius: 4px;"><a href="https://substack.com/redirect/4ba5d82f-c4a5-4a19-a6a4-9cdd6459f54d?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI\_zv\_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g" rel="" style="font-family: system-ui,-apple-system,BlinkMacSystemFont,'Segoe UI',Roboto,Helvetica,Arial,sans-serif,'Apple Color Emoji','Segoe UI Emoji','Segoe UI Symbol';display: inline-block;box-sizing: border-box;border: none;font-size: 14px;line-height: 20px;font-weight: 600;margin: 0;opacity: 1;outline: none;white-space: nowrap;color: #ffffff !important;text-decoration: none !important;text-align: center;cursor: pointer;border-radius: 4px;background-color: #e85c57;padding: 12px 20px;height: auto;"><span style="color: #ffffff;text-decoration: none;">The Social Juice+ (more of what matters)</span></a></p>
<div style="font-size: 16px;line-height: 26px;margin: 32px auto;"><figure style="width: 100%;margin: 0 auto;"><table border="0" cellpadding="0" cellspacing="0" data-component-name="Image2ToDOMStatic" style="mso-padding-alt: 1em 0 1.6em;" width="100%"><tbody><tr>

<td align="left" style="text-align: center;" width="727"><a href="https://substack.com/redirect/1a809d7e-73d5-49dd-bd72-2e0a26f4b8d2?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI\_zv\_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g" rel="" style="position: relative;flex-direction: column;align-items: center;padding: 0;width: auto;height: auto;border: none;text-decoration: none;display: block;margin: 0;" target="\_blank"><img alt="" data-attrs='{"src":"https://substack-post-media.s3.amazonaws.com/public/images/e2e1fbaf-720d-4dbf-a79e-fea6ad235a33\_1100x220.png","srcNoWatermark":null,"fullscreen":null,"imageSize":null,"height":220,"width":1100,"resizeWidth":727,"bytes":50683,"alt":null,"title":null,"type":"image/png","href":null,"belowTheFold":false,"topImage":true,"internalRedirect":"https://thesocialjuice.substack.com/i/163845782?img=https%3A%2F%2Fsubstack-post-media.s3.amazonaws.com%2Fpublic%2Fimages%2Fe2e1fbaf-720d-4dbf-a79e-fea6ad235a33\_1100x220.png","isProcessing":false,"align":null,"offset":false}' height="110" src="https://substackcdn.com/image/fetch/%24s\_!VaNH!,w\_1100,c\_limit,f\_auto,q\_auto:good,fl\_progressive:steep/https%3A%2F%2Fsubstack-post-media.s3.amazonaws.com%2Fpublic%2Fimages%2Fe2e1fbaf-720d-4dbf-a79e-fea6ad235a33\_1100x220.png" style="border: none !important;vertical-align: middle;display: block;-ms-interpolation-mode: bicubic;height: auto;margin-bottom: 0;width: auto !important;max-width: 100% !important;margin: 0 auto;" width="550"/></a></td>
<td style="text-align: center;"></td>
</tr></tbody></table></figure></div>
<ul style="margin-top: 0;padding: 0;">
<li style="margin: 8px 0 0 32px;mso-special-format: bullet;"><p style="color: rgb(54,55,55);line-height: 26px;margin-bottom: 0;box-sizing: border-box;padding-left: 4px;font-size: 16px;margin: 0;"><span>TikTok </span><a href="https://substack.com/redirect/bc404ab1-232c-4d04-9afb-c046787689e1?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI\_zv\_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g" rel="" style="color: #e85c57;text-decoration: none;">launches</a><span> podcast network </span><a href="https://substack.com/redirect/8cae30f5-ea4a-47fe-8c7b-f251e6575147?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI\_zv\_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g" rel="" style="color: #e85c57;text-decoration: none;">with</a><span> iHeartMedia.</span></p></li>
<li style="margin: 8px 0 0 32px;mso-special-format: bullet;"><p style="color: rgb(54,55,55);line-height: 26px;margin-bottom: 0;box-sizing: border-box;padding-left: 4px;font-size: 16px;margin: 0;"><a href="https://substack.com/redirect/00d0756a-8c3f-47fc-87d4-556f0b8934d2?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI\_zv\_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g" rel="" style="color: #e85c57;text-decoration: none;">Google</a><span> is rolling out </span><a href="https://substack.com/redirect/8af80ff1-9556-4150-bf3a-8310f6c253f9?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI\_zv\_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g" rel="" style="color: #e85c57;text-decoration: none;">conversational shopping</a><span>—and </span><a href="https://substack.com/redirect/d84af09a-9dee-464b-b09c-91af858e786c?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI\_zv\_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g" rel="" style="color: #e85c57;text-decoration: none;">ads</a><span>—in AI Mode search.</span></p></li>
<li style="margin: 8px 0 0 32px;mso-special-format: bullet;"><p style="color: rgb(54,55,55);line-height: 26px;margin-bottom: 0;box-sizing: border-box;padding-left: 4px;font-size: 16px;margin: 0;"><span>Disney and YouTube TV have a deal: ESPN and ABC </span><a href="https://substack.com/redirect/fa411ef7-f0d5-4f03-81e0-f6c899b90b5a?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI\_zv\_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g" rel="" style="color: #e85c57;text-decoration: none;">return to streaming service</a><span> after two week blackout.</span></p></li>
<li style="margin: 8px 0 0 32px;mso-special-format: bullet;"><p style="color: rgb(54,55,55);line-height: 26px;margin-bottom: 0;box-sizing: border-box;padding-left: 4px;font-size: 16px;margin: 0;"><span>EU Commission </span><a href="https://substack.com/redirect/d05b32c2-4254-4fcc-8f0a-06ed35d85ae7?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI\_zv\_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g" rel="" style="color: #e85c57;text-decoration: none;">about to</a><span> </span><a href="https://substack.com/redirect/d52376b0-230b-4ea3-826e-77bab2b03011?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI\_zv\_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g" rel="" style="color: #e85c57;text-decoration: none;">wreck</a><span> core principles of the </span><a href="https://substack.com/redirect/81842a66-72d9-4cec-9ef3-016538b564ad?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI\_zv\_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g" rel="" style="color: #e85c57;text-decoration: none;">GDPR</a><span>.</span></p></li>
<li style="margin: 8px 0 0 32px;mso-special-format: bullet;"><p style="color: rgb(54,55,55);line-height: 26px;margin-bottom: 0;box-sizing: border-box;padding-left: 4px;font-size: 16px;margin: 0;"><span>Reddit unveils </span><a href="https://substack.com/redirect/d932f9e2-c6a0-485b-aa0d-58f7170a3aa9?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI\_zv\_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g" rel="" style="color: #e85c57;text-decoration: none;">interactive ad format</a><span>, piloted by </span><a href="https://substack.com/redirect/de275fa1-da4a-431a-92b9-b0c73d850697?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI\_zv\_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g" rel="" style="color: #e85c57;text-decoration: none;">Paramount</a><span>.</span></p></li>
<li style="margin: 8px 0 0 32px;mso-special-format: bullet;"><p style="color: rgb(54,55,55);line-height: 26px;margin-bottom: 0;box-sizing: border-box;padding-left: 4px;font-size: 16px;margin: 0;"><a href="https://substack.com/redirect/1f9a9455-058f-414c-9dbd-fb312ed50c3b?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI\_zv\_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g" rel="" style="color: #e85c57;text-decoration: none;">LinkedIn</a><span> is making it easier to </span><a href="https://substack.com/redirect/33834b84-563c-4424-a9c7-e5bc45de86c8?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI\_zv\_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g" rel="" style="color: #e85c57;text-decoration: none;">search for people with AI</a><span>.</span></p></li>
</ul>
<div style="font-size: 16px;line-height: 26px;"><hr style="margin: 32px 0;padding: 0;height: 1px;background: #e6e6e6;border: none;"/></div>
<div style="font-size: 16px;line-height: 26px;margin: 32px auto;"><figure style="width: 100%;margin: 0 auto;"><table border="0" cellpadding="0" cellspacing="0" data-component-name="Image2ToDOMStatic" style="mso-padding-alt: 1em 0 1.6em;" width="100%"><tbody><tr>
<td style="text-align: center;"></td>
<td align="left" style="text-align: center;" width="727"><a href="https://substack.com/redirect/f7eb20a6-e7e8-4300-a972-6846526caf8c?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI\_zv\_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g" rel="" style="position: relative;flex-direction: column;align-items: center;padding: 0;width: auto;height: auto;border: none;text-decoration: none;display: block;margin: 0;" target="\_blank"><img alt="" data-attrs='{"src":"https://substack-post-media.s3.amazonaws.com/public/images/484d6c9a-ab2b-4a12-b322-17f701d13b66\_1100x220.png","srcNoWatermark":null,"fullscreen":null,"imageSize":null,"height":220,"width":1100,"resizeWidth":727,"bytes":48232,"alt":null,"title":null,"type":"image/png","href":null,"belowTheFold":false,"topImage":false,"internalRedirect":"https://thesocialjuice.substack.com/i/163845782?img=https%3A%2F%2Fsubstack-post-media.s3.amazonaws.com%2Fpublic%2Fimages%2F484d6c9a-ab2b-4a12-b322-17f701d13b66\_1100x220.png","isProcessing":false,"align":null,"offset":false}' height="110" src="https://substackcdn.com/image/fetch/%24s\_!zM4F!,w\_1100,c\_limit,f\_auto,q\_auto:good,fl\_progressive:steep/https%3A%2F%2Fsubstack-post-media.s3.amazonaws.com%2Fpublic%2Fimages%2F484d6c9a-ab2b-4a12-b322-17f701d13b66\_1100x220.png" style="border: none !important;vertical-align: middle;display: block;-ms-interpolation-mode: bicubic;height: auto;margin-bottom: 0;width: auto !important;max-width: 100% !important;margin: 0 auto;" width="550"/></a></td>
<td style="text-align: center;"></td>
</tr></tbody></table></figure></div>
<ul style="margin-top: 0;padding: 0;">
<li style="margin: 8px 0 0 32px;mso-special-format: bullet;"><p style="color: rgb(54,55,55);line-height: 26px;margin-bottom: 0;box-sizing: border-box;padding-left: 4px;font-size: 16px;margin: 0;"><a href="https://substack.com/redirect/2fc148f7-e7b9-4575-b4d1-6b05b4041892?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI\_zv\_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g" rel="" style="color: #e85c57;text-decoration: none;">Vine is back</a><span>, sort of: New app </span><a href="https://substack.com/redirect/93b579c8-a402-44c5-ad1c-571af186c988?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI\_zv\_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g" rel="" style="color: #e85c57;text-decoration: none;">shuts out AI</a><span> as thousands of old videos return.</span></p></li>
<li style="margin: 8px 0 0 32px;mso-special-format: bullet;">
<p style="margin: 0 0 20px 0;color: rgb(54,55,55);line-height: 26px;margin-bottom: 0;box-sizing: border-box;padding-left: 4px;font-size: 16px;"><span>Sky Sports axes woman-focused </span><a href="https://substack.com/redirect/54268993-fd2c-4b24-8acc-c09cd0e17483?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI\_zv\_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g" rel="" style="color: #e85c57;text-decoration: none;">TikTok channel Halo</a><span> following fierce backlash.</span></p>
<ul style="margin-top: 0;padding: 0;"><li style="margin: 8px 0 0 32px;mso-special-format: bullet;"><p style="color: rgb(54,55,55);line-height: 26px;margin-bottom: 0;box-sizing: border-box;padding-left: 4px;font-size: 16px;margin: 0;">This was a disaster and possibly one of the worst TikTok marketing decisions ever.</p></li></ul>
</li>
<li style="margin: 8px 0 0 32px;mso-special-format: bullet;">
<p style="margin: 0 0 20px 0;color: rgb(54,55,55);line-height: 26px;margin-bottom: 0;box-sizing: border-box;padding-left: 4px;font-size: 16px;"><a href="https://substack.com/redirect/3e886a14-f5cb-404e-a45b-56875a5a4279?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI\_zv\_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g" rel="" style="color: #e85c57;text-decoration: none;">Former CEO of WPP</a><span> that produces ‘Love Island’ sues </span><a href="https://substack.com/redirect/4d330c43-7208-4d49-88a9-c6d13add1903?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI\_zv\_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g" rel="" style="color: #e85c57;text-decoration: none;">ad agency</a><span> for $100 million.</span></p>
<ul style="margin-top: 0;padding: 0;"><li style="margin: 8px 0 0 32px;mso-special-format: bullet;">
<p style="margin: 0 0 20px 0;color: rgb(54,55,55);line-height: 26px;margin-bottom: 0;box-sizing: border-box;padding-left: 4px;font-size: 16px;"><span>WPP draws </span><a href="https://substack.com/redirect/5bfd170c-03cb-4690-9df0-1792bd75de4f?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI\_zv\_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g" rel="" style="color: #e85c57;text-decoration: none;">interest</a><span> from Havas and private equity, Times says.</span></p>
<ul style="margin-top: 0;padding: 0;"><li style="margin: 8px 0 0 32px;mso-special-format: bullet;"><p style="color: rgb(54,55,55);line-height: 26px;margin-bottom: 0;box-sizing: border-box;padding-left: 4px;font-size: 16px;margin: 0;"><span>WPP brings in </span><a href="https://substack.com/redirect/b08bfb74-b270-4989-b3a5-af5bb400314c?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI\_zv\_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g" rel="" style="color: #e85c57;text-decoration: none;">McKinsey</a><span> to advise on strategic review.</span></p></li></ul>
</li></ul>
</li>
<li style="margin: 8px 0 0 32px;mso-special-format: bullet;"><p style="color: rgb(54,55,55);line-height: 26px;margin-bottom: 0;box-sizing: border-box;padding-left: 4px;font-size: 16px;margin: 0;"><span>The age of </span><a href="https://substack.com/redirect/a005e863-26fe-4877-b627-2954748f6060?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI\_zv\_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g" rel="" style="color: #e85c57;text-decoration: none;">anti-social media</a><span> is here</span></p></li>
<li style="margin: 8px 0 0 32px;mso-special-format: bullet;"><p style="color: rgb(54,55,55);line-height: 26px;margin-bottom: 0;box-sizing: border-box;padding-left: 4px;font-size: 16px;margin: 0;"><span>Snapchat is </span><a href="https://substack.com/redirect/a38c40f4-269d-47fd-9a44-13ac57af875b?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI\_zv\_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g" rel="" style="color: #e85c57;text-decoration: none;">bringing back</a><span> classic 2D Bitmoji.</span></p></li>
<li style="margin: 8px 0 0 32px;mso-special-format: bullet;"><p style="color: rgb(54,55,55);line-height: 26px;margin-bottom: 0;box-sizing: border-box;padding-left: 4px;font-size: 16px;margin: 0;"><span>EU launches antitrust probe into how </span><a href="https://substack.com/redirect/280fc4c9-a885-4f28-bdb4-4b94d32f55bb?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI\_zv\_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g" rel="" style="color: #e85c57;text-decoration: none;">Google’s anti-spam policy</a><span> affects publishers’ search rankings.</span></p></li>
<li style="margin: 8px 0 0 32px;mso-special-format: bullet;"><p style="color: rgb(54,55,55);line-height: 26px;margin-bottom: 0;box-sizing: border-box;padding-left: 4px;font-size: 16px;margin: 0;"><a href="https://substack.com/redirect/bb9948c0-e6c6-443c-839d-d6b4fb9365c6?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI\_zv\_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g" rel="" style="color: #e85c57;text-decoration: none;">AI slop hits new high</a><span> as fake country artist goes to #1 on Billboard digital songs chart.</span></p></li>
<li style="margin: 8px 0 0 32px;mso-special-format: bullet;"><p style="color: rgb(54,55,55);line-height: 26px;margin-bottom: 0;box-sizing: border-box;padding-left: 4px;font-size: 16px;margin: 0;"><span>Paramount+ is growing up, and executives have decided </span><a href="https://substack.com/redirect/eb182acf-bf46-4b34-84a1-b7d976978bab?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI\_zv\_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g" rel="" style="color: #e85c57;text-decoration: none;">it’s time to retire its free trial</a><span>.</span></p></li>
</ul>
<div style="font-size: 16px;line-height: 26px;"><hr style="margin: 32px 0;padding: 0;height: 1px;background: #e6e6e6;border: none;"/></div>
<blockquote style="border-left: 4px solid #e85c57;margin: 20px 0;padding: 0;"><p style="margin: 0 0 20px 0;color: rgb(54,55,55);margin-left: 20px;line-height: 26px;font-size: 16px;"><em><span>In partnership with </span><a href="https://substack.com/redirect/21419bca-21df-4c5c-91bd-a10105f21abf?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI\_zv\_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g" rel="" style="color: #e85c57;text-decoration: none;">Tracksuit</a><span> </span></em></p></blockquote>
<blockquote style="border-left: 4px solid #e85c57;margin: 20px 0;padding: 0;">
<p style="margin: 0 0 20px 0;color: rgb(54,55,55);margin-left: 20px;line-height: 26px;font-size: 16px;"><span>Marketers are under the pump to go big or go home. They need to perform, entertain, grow </span><em>and</em><span> </span><a href="https://substack.com/redirect/21419bca-21df-4c5c-91bd-a10105f21abf?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI\_zv\_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g" rel="" style="color: #e85c57;text-decoration: none;">prove</a><span> the ROI of brand spend.</span></p>
<p style="margin: 0 0 20px 0;color: rgb(54,55,55);margin-left: 20px;line-height: 26px;font-size: 16px;">If you’re trying to take a big bet instead of creating the same cookie cutter campaigns each quarter, you need evidence it improved awareness or shifted perception in your key audience segments — not just reached a million people.</p>
<p style="margin: 0 0 20px 0;color: rgb(54,55,55);margin-left: 20px;line-height: 26px;font-size: 16px;"><a href="https://substack.com/redirect/21419bca-21df-4c5c-91bd-a10105f21abf?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI\_zv\_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g" rel="" style="color: #e85c57;text-decoration: none;">Tracksuit’s </a><span>always-on brand tracking that helps you prove the impact of your boldest campaign work over time…so you’ll </span><a href="https://substack.com/redirect/21419bca-21df-4c5c-91bd-a10105f21abf?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI\_zv\_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g" rel="" style="color: #e85c57;text-decoration: none;">get the budget</a><span> for the next moderately chaotic idea.</span></p>
</blockquote>
<div style="font-size: 16px;line-height: 26px;"><hr style="margin: 32px 0;padding: 0;height: 1px;background: #e6e6e6;border: none;"/></div>
<ul style="margin-top: 0;padding: 0;">
<li style="margin: 8px 0 0 32px;mso-special-format: bullet;"><p style="color: rgb(54,55,55);line-height: 26px;margin-bottom: 0;box-sizing: border-box;padding-left: 4px;font-size: 16px;margin: 0;"><span>Amid search wars, </span><a href="https://substack.com/redirect/b45ed81b-644b-4d29-a9aa-243646082de5?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI\_zv\_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g" rel="" style="color: #e85c57;text-decoration: none;">Google touts YouTube</a><span>, display inventory to advertisers. </span></p></li>
<li style="margin: 8px 0 0 32px;mso-special-format: bullet;"><p style="color: rgb(54,55,55);line-height: 26px;margin-bottom: 0;box-sizing: border-box;padding-left: 4px;font-size: 16px;margin: 0;"><span>Brands tap </span><a href="https://substack.com/redirect/c2db290e-bd1a-4402-852d-a2166f525f11?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI\_zv\_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g" rel="" style="color: #e85c57;text-decoration: none;">outside-the-box personalities</a><span> to stand out as influencer marketing gets more competitive.</span></p></li>
<li style="margin: 8px 0 0 32px;mso-special-format: bullet;"><p style="color: rgb(54,55,55);line-height: 26px;margin-bottom: 0;box-sizing: border-box;padding-left: 4px;font-size: 16px;margin: 0;"><span>Amazon Prime Video </span><a href="https://substack.com/redirect/15fc0794-9c6c-4bed-ae07-324ac9acbc72?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI\_zv\_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g" rel="" style="color: #e85c57;text-decoration: none;">ad-supported reach hits 315M</a><span> monthly viewers.</span></p></li>
<li style="margin: 8px 0 0 32px;mso-special-format: bullet;"><p style="color: rgb(54,55,55);line-height: 26px;margin-bottom: 0;box-sizing: border-box;padding-left: 4px;font-size: 16px;margin: 0;"><span>Michael Caine and Matthew McConaughey partner with </span><a href="https://substack.com/redirect/f64569c9-cb0d-4683-90cb-35bc593c01d8?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI\_zv\_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g" rel="" style="color: #e85c57;text-decoration: none;">ElevenLabs</a><span> for AI voice cloning.</span></p></li>
<li style="margin: 8px 0 0 32px;mso-special-format: bullet;"><p style="color: rgb(54,55,55);line-height: 26px;margin-bottom: 0;box-sizing: border-box;padding-left: 4px;font-size: 16px;margin: 0;"><span>The </span><a href="https://substack.com/redirect/f3ee2b08-bc60-463e-a77d-f4fcb3bf6b36?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI\_zv\_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g" rel="" style="color: #e85c57;text-decoration: none;">fight to save</a><span> the $2.7 billion botox empire.</span></p></li>
<li style="margin: 8px 0 0 32px;mso-special-format: bullet;">
<p style="margin: 0 0 20px 0;color: rgb(54,55,55);line-height: 26px;margin-bottom: 0;box-sizing: border-box;padding-left: 4px;font-size: 16px;"><span>In Trump 2.0, MAGA-aligned influencers and media emerge as </span><a href="https://substack.com/redirect/a957af20-f5fb-49fa-bcfe-dc03779d794f?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI\_zv\_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g" rel="" style="color: #e85c57;text-decoration: none;">the new mainstream</a><span>.</span></p>
<ul style="margin-top: 0;padding: 0;"><li style="margin: 8px 0 0 32px;mso-special-format: bullet;"><p style="color: rgb(54,55,55);line-height: 26px;margin-bottom: 0;box-sizing: border-box;padding-left: 4px;font-size: 16px;margin: 0;"><span>Influencers are </span><a href="https://substack.com/redirect/3dce8d4b-4442-401a-abfc-0fd1ae9d5192?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI\_zv\_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g" rel="" style="color: #e85c57;text-decoration: none;">royalty</a><span> at this college, and the turf war is vicious.</span></p></li></ul>
</li>
<li style="margin: 8px 0 0 32px;mso-special-format: bullet;"><p style="color: rgb(54,55,55);line-height: 26px;margin-bottom: 0;box-sizing: border-box;padding-left: 4px;font-size: 16px;margin: 0;"><span>‘</span><a href="https://substack.com/redirect/e9081fe9-c757-4b1d-8b74-cbc42122fb89?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI\_zv\_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g" rel="" style="color: #e85c57;text-decoration: none;">Beast Land</a><span>’ opens in Saudi Arabia: Everything </span><a href="https://substack.com/redirect/360dced7-b5ae-4811-90ce-6b89a9bdd3e1?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI\_zv\_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g" rel="" style="color: #e85c57;text-decoration: none;">we know</a><span> about MrBeast’s new theme park.</span></p></li>
<li style="margin: 8px 0 0 32px;mso-special-format: bullet;"><p style="color: rgb(54,55,55);line-height: 26px;margin-bottom: 0;box-sizing: border-box;padding-left: 4px;font-size: 16px;margin: 0;"><span>Who pays when </span><a href="https://substack.com/redirect/f8d3c0fe-7fb0-474d-bd85-3d2545fe422c?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI\_zv\_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g" rel="" style="color: #e85c57;text-decoration: none;">A.I. is wrong</a><span>?</span></p></li>
<li style="margin: 8px 0 0 32px;mso-special-format: bullet;"><p style="color: rgb(54,55,55);line-height: 26px;margin-bottom: 0;box-sizing: border-box;padding-left: 4px;font-size: 16px;margin: 0;"><span>Arthur Sadoun urges Omnicom to </span><a href="https://substack.com/redirect/8832015c-17f7-4d82-a1f5-dbb100d2c2a1?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI\_zv\_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g" rel="" style="color: #e85c57;text-decoration: none;">report financials</a><span> like the other big four holdcos.</span></p></li>
<li style="margin: 8px 0 0 32px;mso-special-format: bullet;"><p style="color: rgb(54,55,55);line-height: 26px;margin-bottom: 0;box-sizing: border-box;padding-left: 4px;font-size: 16px;margin: 0;"><a href="https://substack.com/redirect/bd045a9d-78a8-4792-b084-3c9c8714a113?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI\_zv\_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g" rel="" style="color: #e85c57;text-decoration: none;">Steam Machine</a><span>: Valve </span><a href="https://substack.com/redirect/c0622505-553f-4ec3-a1f9-94710e8432e5?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI\_zv\_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g" rel="" style="color: #e85c57;text-decoration: none;">rivals</a><span> Xbox and PlayStation with new console.</span></p></li>
</ul>
<div style="font-size: 16px;line-height: 26px;margin: 32px auto;"><figure style="width: 100%;margin: 0 auto;"><table border="0" cellpadding="0" cellspacing="0" data-component-name="Image2ToDOMStatic" style="mso-padding-alt: 1em 0 1.6em;" width="100%"><tbody><tr>
<td style="text-align: center;"></td>
<td align="left" style="text-align: center;" width="727"><a href="https://substack.com/redirect/a7e85e66-1417-480b-adfa-0928cd5d6574?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI\_zv\_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g" rel="" style="position: relative;flex-direction: column;align-items: center;padding: 0;width: auto;height: auto;border: none;text-decoration: none;display: block;margin: 0;" target="\_blank"><img alt="" data-attrs='{"src":"https://substack-post-media.s3.amazonaws.com/public/images/6a655c94-ab41-4b83-8ee6-37cf93a7d7bb\_1100x220.png","srcNoWatermark":null,"fullscreen":null,"imageSize":null,"height":220,"width":1100,"resizeWidth":727,"bytes":53060,"alt":null,"title":null,"type":"image/png","href":null,"belowTheFold":true,"topImage":false,"internalRedirect":"https://thesocialjuice.substack.com/i/163845782?img=https%3A%2F%2Fsubstack-post-media.s3.amazonaws.com%2Fpublic%2Fimages%2F6a655c94-ab41-4b83-8ee6-37cf93a7d7bb\_1100x220.png","isProcessing":false,"align":null,"offset":false}' height="110" src="https://substackcdn.com/image/fetch/%24s\_!BaXH!,w\_1100,c\_limit,f\_auto,q\_auto:good,fl\_progressive:steep/https%3A%2F%2Fsubstack-post-media.s3.amazonaws.com%2Fpublic%2Fimages%2F6a655c94-ab41-4b83-8ee6-37cf93a7d7bb\_1100x220.png" style="border: none !important;vertical-align: middle;display: block;-ms-interpolation-mode: bicubic;height: auto;margin-bottom: 0;width: auto !important;max-width: 100% !important;margin: 0 auto;" width="550"/></a></td>
<td style="text-align: center;"></td>
</tr></tbody></table></figure></div>
<ul style="margin-top: 0;padding: 0;">
<li style="margin: 8px 0 0 32px;mso-special-format: bullet;">
<p style="margin: 0 0 20px 0;color: rgb(54,55,55);line-height: 26px;margin-bottom: 0;box-sizing: border-box;padding-left: 4px;font-size: 16px;"><span>DIY Disney? The company is </span><a href="https://substack.com/redirect/ea24bf31-e9b1-4061-ac96-84ab740f95b5?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI\_zv\_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g" rel="" style="color: #e85c57;text-decoration: none;">exploring AI</a><span> so fans can make AI slop content from Disney stories.</span></p>
<ul style="margin-top: 0;padding: 0;"><li style="margin: 8px 0 0 32px;mso-special-format: bullet;"><p style="color: rgb(54,55,55);line-height: 26px;margin-bottom: 0;box-sizing: border-box;padding-left: 4px;font-size: 16px;margin: 0;"><span>Disney to </span><a href="https://substack.com/redirect/d34644ff-a96b-41f4-8a60-096e7b94343d?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI\_zv\_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g" rel="" style="color: #e85c57;text-decoration: none;">increase content spending</a><span> by $1 billion next year.</span></p></li></ul>
</li>
<li style="margin: 8px 0 0 32px;mso-special-format: bullet;"><p style="color: rgb(54,55,55);line-height: 26px;margin-bottom: 0;box-sizing: border-box;padding-left: 4px;font-size: 16px;margin: 0;"><span>EU: Chat Control </span><a href="https://substack.com/redirect/08e42046-437f-4d3e-ab42-c6dd87e6400c?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI\_zv\_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g" rel="" style="color: #e85c57;text-decoration: none;">isn’t</a><span> dead, </span><a href="https://substack.com/redirect/36e8534f-22bb-4971-a435-abd14f794a47?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI\_zv\_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g" rel="" style="color: #e85c57;text-decoration: none;">Denmark</a><span> has a new proposal.</span></p></li>
<li style="margin: 8px 0 0 32px;mso-special-format: bullet;"><p style="color: rgb(54,55,55);line-height: 26px;margin-bottom: 0;box-sizing: border-box;padding-left: 4px;font-size: 16px;margin: 0;"><span>Under Armour splits with </span><a href="https://substack.com/redirect/c98f5d04-b4eb-4315-aa18-824f5c7205e6?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI\_zv\_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g" rel="" style="color: #e85c57;text-decoration: none;">Steph Curry</a><span>, leaving NBA legend free to find a new business partner.</span></p></li>
<li style="margin: 8px 0 0 32px;mso-special-format: bullet;"><p style="color: rgb(54,55,55);line-height: 26px;margin-bottom: 0;box-sizing: border-box;padding-left: 4px;font-size: 16px;margin: 0;"><span>The viral TikToks </span><a href="https://substack.com/redirect/6e0944fa-4f25-45f0-b475-4c22d217f52d?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI\_zv\_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g" rel="" style="color: #e85c57;text-decoration: none;">testing</a><span> churches.</span></p></li>
<li style="margin: 8px 0 0 32px;mso-special-format: bullet;"><p style="color: rgb(54,55,55);line-height: 26px;margin-bottom: 0;box-sizing: border-box;padding-left: 4px;font-size: 16px;margin: 0;"><span>J.P. Morgan calls out </span><a href="https://substack.com/redirect/8f39b219-afee-4724-8248-e93454870571?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI\_zv\_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g" rel="" style="color: #e85c57;text-decoration: none;">AI spend</a><span>, says $650 billion in </span><a href="https://substack.com/redirect/7ca3a7ec-a4cf-4bd8-ac88-92170f12552d?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI\_zv\_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g" rel="" style="color: #e85c57;text-decoration: none;">annual revenue required</a><span> to deliver mere 10% return on AI buildout.</span></p></li>
<li style="margin: 8px 0 0 32px;mso-special-format: bullet;"><p style="color: rgb(54,55,55);line-height: 26px;margin-bottom: 0;box-sizing: border-box;padding-left: 4px;font-size: 16px;margin: 0;"><span>It’s official. </span><a href="https://substack.com/redirect/1f3a7103-0b2d-459b-8840-b8a926a8a6a1?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI\_zv\_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g" rel="" style="color: #e85c57;text-decoration: none;">Sonder</a><span> files for bankruptcy </span><a href="https://substack.com/redirect/8a56ca71-aff0-4423-bd04-db4a06f5d923?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI\_zv\_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g" rel="" style="color: #e85c57;text-decoration: none;">after</a><span> its Marriott breakup.</span></p></li>

</li></ul>
</li>

<li style="margin: 8px 0 0 32px;mso-special-format: bullet;"><p style="color: rgb(54,55,55);line-height: 26px;margin-bottom: 0;box-sizing: border-box;padding-left: 4px;font-size: 16px;margin: 0;"><span>Google Ads boosts accuracy in </span><a href="https://substack.com/redirect/7fe6ecca-112d-4369-93f6-2e697e7a1fd9?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI\_zv\_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g" rel="" style="color: #e85c57;text-decoration: none;">advertiser account suspensions</a><span>.</span></p></li>
<li style="margin: 8px 0 0 32px;mso-special-format: bullet;">
<p style="margin: 0 0 20px 0;color: rgb(54,55,55);line-height: 26px;margin-bottom: 0;box-sizing: border-box;padding-left: 4px;font-size: 16px;"><span>Google </span><a href="https://substack.com/redirect/ceede53e-5e4e-44a6-90b2-3bd6f924e289?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI\_zv\_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g" rel="" style="color: #e85c57;text-decoration: none;">snubs</a><span> EU request to break itself up.</span></p>
<ul style="margin-top: 0;padding: 0;"><li style="margin: 8px 0 0 32px;mso-special-format: bullet;"><p style="color: rgb(54,55,55);line-height: 26px;margin-bottom: 0;box-sizing: border-box;padding-left: 4px;font-size: 16px;margin: 0;"><span>Google offers</span><a href="https://substack.com/redirect/6245ddde-8366-442a-a866-00efe5bd39ca?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI\_zv\_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g" rel="" style="color: #e85c57;text-decoration: none;"> EU to change adtech policy</a><span>, no divestment.</span></p></li></ul>
</li>
<li style="margin: 8px 0 0 32px;mso-special-format: bullet;"><p style="color: rgb(54,55,55);line-height: 26px;margin-bottom: 0;box-sizing: border-box;padding-left: 4px;font-size: 16px;margin: 0;"><a href="https://substack.com/redirect/5492f00c-029a-4a3d-84fc-d57b1a13a6b6?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI\_zv\_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g" rel="" style="color: #e85c57;text-decoration: none;">South Korea</a><span> delays decision on Google’s request to export map data.</span></p></li>
<li style="margin: 8px 0 0 32px;mso-special-format: bullet;">
<p style="margin: 0 0 20px 0;color: rgb(54,55,55);line-height: 26px;margin-bottom: 0;box-sizing: border-box;padding-left: 4px;font-size: 16px;"><span>Google plans </span><a href="https://substack.com/redirect/d118003d-1aed-48c9-86f3-8d4a7177145c?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI\_zv\_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g" rel="" style="color: #e85c57;text-decoration: none;">$40 billion Texas data center</a><span> investment amid AI boom.</span></p>
<ul style="margin-top: 0;padding: 0;"><li style="margin: 8px 0 0 32px;mso-special-format: bullet;"><p style="color: rgb(54,55,55);line-height: 26px;margin-bottom: 0;box-sizing: border-box;padding-left: 4px;font-size: 16px;margin: 0;"><span>Google is still aiming for its “</span><a href="https://substack.com/redirect/f13b52a1-bfc8-4a51-a9ba-e4f646bc2857?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI\_zv\_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g" rel="" style="color: #e85c57;text-decoration: none;">moonshot</a><span>” 2030 energy goals.</span></p></li></ul>
</li>
<li style="margin: 8px 0 0 32px;mso-special-format: bullet;"><p style="color: rgb(54,55,55);line-height: 26px;margin-bottom: 0;box-sizing: border-box;padding-left: 4px;font-size: 16px;margin: 0;"><span>Google </span><a href="https://substack.com/redirect/cea41474-8696-4287-84aa-d48942d05314?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI\_zv\_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g" rel="" style="color: #e85c57;text-decoration: none;">launches</a><span> a lawsuit targeting text message scammers.</span></p></li>
<li style="margin: 8px 0 0 32px;mso-special-format: bullet;"><p style="color: rgb(54,55,55);line-height: 26px;margin-bottom: 0;box-sizing: border-box;padding-left: 4px;font-size: 16px;margin: 0;"><span>Google </span><a href="https://substack.com/redirect/3d2fe407-97c7-4872-834c-cf11fe4c56a4?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI\_zv\_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g" rel="" style="color: #e85c57;text-decoration: none;">launches</a><span> Gemini-powered AI Agents for advertising and analytics.</span></p></li>

<ul style="margin-top: 0;padding: 0;">
<li style="margin: 8px 0 0 32px;mso-special-format: bullet;"><p style="color: rgb(54,55,55);line-height: 26px;margin-bottom: 0;box-sizing: border-box;padding-left: 4px;font-size: 16px;margin: 0;"><span>X will show a warning on user accounts if </span><a href="https://substack.com/redirect/215bcf54-463f-418f-8c0b-6ff3eb026e4f?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI\_zv\_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g" rel="" style="color: #e85c57;text-decoration: none;">someone tries to use a VPN</a><span> to hide their account location when the upcoming ‘About Your Account’ feature launches.</span></p></li>
<li style="margin: 8px 0 0 32px;mso-special-format: bullet;"><p style="color: rgb(54,55,55);line-height: 26px;margin-bottom: 0;box-sizing: border-box;padding-left: 4px;font-size: 16px;margin: 0;"><span>Irish media regulator opens </span><a href="https://substack.com/redirect/4ddc4805-5b7a-4959-87f8-733b6def4336?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI\_zv\_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g" rel="" style="color: #e85c57;text-decoration: none;">investigation</a><span> into </span><a href="https://substack.com/redirect/e14bb300-eae8-4fab-94eb-4b33632e1f18?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI\_zv\_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g" rel="" style="color: #e85c57;text-decoration: none;">Musk’s X</a><span>.</span></p></li>
<li style="margin: 8px 0 0 32px;mso-special-format: bullet;">
<p style="margin: 0 0 20px 0;color: rgb(54,55,55);line-height: 26px;margin-bottom: 0;box-sizing: border-box;padding-left: 4px;font-size: 16px;"><span>X rolls out its Signal-like </span><a href="https://substack.com/redirect/f36bde99-f6b4-4a1e-88c7-5fb8dfd7168a?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI\_zv\_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g" rel="" style="color: #e85c57;text-decoration: none;">encrypted chat</a><span> feature.</span></p>
<ul style="margin-top: 0;padding: 0;"><li style="margin: 8px 0 0 32px;mso-special-format: bullet;"><p style="color: rgb(54,55,55);line-height: 26px;margin-bottom: 0;box-sizing: border-box;padding-left: 4px;font-size: 16px;margin: 0;"><span>Users </span><a href="https://substack.com/redirect/32e818b9-063e-423b-b580-6526a431bb70?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI\_zv\_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g" rel="" style="color: #e85c57;text-decoration: none;">criticize</a><span> new encrypted chat feature that replaces direct messages.</span></p></li></ul>
</li>
<li style="margin: 8px 0 0 32px;mso-special-format: bullet;"><p style="color: rgb(54,55,55);line-height: 26px;margin-bottom: 0;box-sizing: border-box;padding-left: 4px;font-size: 16px;margin: 0;"><span>Elon Musk says report on xAI’s $15 billion fundraise is ‘</span><a href="https://substack.com/redirect/f9f85faa-ca38-401a-a0a4-e2cdc36100e0?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI\_zv\_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g" rel="" style="color: #e85c57;text-decoration: none;">false</a><span>’.</span></p></li>
<li style="margin: 8px 0 0 32px;mso-special-format: bullet;"><p style="color: rgb(54,55,55);line-height: 26px;margin-bottom: 0;box-sizing: border-box;padding-left: 4px;font-size: 16px;margin: 0;"><span>X will soon let you </span><a href="https://substack.com/redirect/fd0847b8-195e-4a7d-ab28-1caf43bf93f4?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI\_zv\_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g" rel="" style="color: #e85c57;text-decoration: none;">sort your following feed</a><span> by “popular”.</span></p></li>
<li style="margin: 8px 0 0 32px;mso-special-format: bullet;"><p style="color: rgb(54,55,55);line-height: 26px;margin-bottom: 0;box-sizing: border-box;padding-left: 4px;font-size: 16px;margin: 0;"><span>Elon Musk’s Grok AI briefly says </span><a href="https://substack.com/redirect/59783f6c-c030-4205-8891-41d513e6686c?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI\_zv\_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g" rel="" style="color: #e85c57;text-decoration: none;">Trump won 2020</a><span> presidential election.</span></p></li>
<li style="margin: 8px 0 0 32px;mso-special-format: bullet;"><p style="color: rgb(54,55,55);line-height: 26px;margin-bottom: 0;box-sizing: border-box;padding-left: 4px;font-size: 16px;margin: 0;"><span>X adds </span><a href="https://substack.com/redirect/17d53da2-5c12-4096-bd63-9a3212dc7843?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI\_zv\_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g" rel="" style="color: #e85c57;text-decoration: none;">new parameters</a><span> for buying handles.</span></p></li>
<li style="margin: 8px 0 0 32px;mso-special-format: bullet;"><p style="color: rgb(54,55,55);line-height: 26px;margin-bottom: 0;box-sizing: border-box;padding-left: 4px;font-size: 16px;margin: 0;"><span>xAI delays </span><a href="https://substack.com/redirect/197f6478-d271-4a25-ac44-1cb2d9ee004e?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI\_zv\_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g" rel="" style="color: #e85c57;text-decoration: none;">Grok 5 release</a><span> to next year.</span></p></li>
<li style="margin: 8px 0 0 32px;mso-special-format: bullet;"><p style="color: rgb(54,55,55);line-height: 26px;margin-bottom: 0;box-sizing: border-box;padding-left: 4px;font-size: 16px;margin: 0;"><span>Apple and OpenAI </span><a href="https://substack.com/redirect/2e57105e-7277-4881-9a4d-9600e8c8fccc?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI\_zv\_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g" rel="" style="color: #e85c57;text-decoration: none;">fail</a><span> to dismiss </span><a href="https://substack.com/redirect/a07119c7-efa0-4f78-bf39-82c1c041a662?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI\_zv\_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g" rel="" style="color: #e85c57;text-decoration: none;">Elon Musk’s antitrust lawsuit</a><span>.</span></p></li>
<li style="margin: 8px 0 0 32px;mso-special-format: bullet;"><p style="color: rgb(54,55,55);line-height: 26px;margin-bottom: 0;box-sizing: border-box;padding-left: 4px;font-size: 16px;margin: 0;"><span>X appears to be </span><a href="https://substack.com/redirect/cd968ddf-71da-4f38-a456-65d58bd04da4?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI\_zv\_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g" rel="" style="color: #e85c57;text-decoration: none;">sending fake traffic</a><span> across the web.</span></p></li>
<li style="margin: 8px 0 0 32px;mso-special-format: bullet;"><p style="color: rgb(54,55,55);line-height: 26px;margin-bottom: 0;box-sizing: border-box;padding-left: 4px;font-size: 16px;margin: 0;"><span>Spain fines X </span><a href="https://substack.com/redirect/ea3a25e4-3538-454e-8ceb-70cf6442e2f7?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI\_zv\_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g" rel="" style="color: #e85c57;text-decoration: none;">$5.8 million</a><span> in cryptoasset advertising case.</span></p></li>
<li style="margin: 8px 0 0 32px;mso-special-format: bullet;"><p style="color: rgb(54,55,55);line-height: 26px;margin-bottom: 0;box-sizing: border-box;padding-left: 4px;font-size: 16px;margin: 0;"><span>X </span><a href="https://substack.com/redirect/88175141-391d-4e16-9cc5-ddf24c723967?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI\_zv\_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g" rel="" style="color: #e85c57;text-decoration: none;">awards</a><span> first set of ‘Certified Banger’ posts.</span></p></li>
<li style="margin: 8px 0 0 32px;mso-special-format: bullet;"><p style="color: rgb(54,55,55);line-height: 26px;margin-bottom: 0;box-sizing: border-box;padding-left: 4px;font-size: 16px;margin: 0;"><span>xAI works on </span><a href="https://substack.com/redirect/94ffbdbb-0d13-416b-ba53-85b484474308?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI\_zv\_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g" rel="" style="color: #e85c57;text-decoration: none;">Grok Code Remote</a><span> to rival OpenAI’s Codex.</span></p></li>
</ul>
<blockquote style="border-left: 4px solid #e85c57;margin: 20px 0;padding: 0;"><p style="margin: 0 0 20px 0;color: rgb(54,55,55);margin-left: 20px;line-height: 26px;font-size: 16px;"><span>🥀 Report Break: </span><a href="https://substack.com/redirect/59e33f48-b7e1-410a-9493-a85afc3c480a?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI\_zv\_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g" rel="" style="color: #e85c57;text-decoration: none;">Insiders promoted to the CEO role</a><span> consistently outperform outside hires, according to a new study by the Yale School of Management. “It’s not even close,” said Professor Jeffrey Sonnenfeld, who conducted the research into the past five years’ Fortune 500 appointments with his colleague Steven Tian.</span></p></blockquote>

<td style="text-align: center;"></td>
<td align="left" style="text-align: center;" width="727"><a href="https://substack.com/redirect/faa56247-32a5-433c-b681-9cc854dc2f8a?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI\_zv\_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g" rel="" style="position: relative;flex-direction: column;align-items: center;padding: 0;width: auto;height: auto;border: none;text-decoration: none;display: block;margin: 0;" target="\_blank"><img alt="" data-attrs='{"src":"https://substack-post-media.s3.amazonaws.com/public/images/20a7b272-bbeb-4fe3-a619-d76e41822174\_1100x220.png","srcNoWatermark":null,"fullscreen":null,"imageSize":null,"height":220,"width":1100,"resizeWidth":727,"bytes":42259,"alt":null,"title":null,"type":"image/png","href":null,"belowTheFold":true,"topImage":false,"internalRedirect":"https://thesocialjuice.substack.com/i/163845782?img=https%3A%2F%2Fsubstack-post-media.s3.amazonaws.com%2Fpublic%2Fimages%2F20a7b272-bbeb-4fe3-a619-d76e41822174\_1100x220.png","isProcessing":false,"align":null,"offset":false}' height="110" src="https://substackcdn.com/image/fetch/%24s\_!85wj!,w\_1100,c\_limit,f\_auto,q\_auto:good,fl\_progressive:steep/https%3A%2F%2Fsubstack-post-media.s3.amazonaws.com%2Fpublic%2Fimages%2F20a7b272-bbeb-4fe3-a619-d76e41822174\_1100x220.png" style="border: none !important;vertical-align: middle;display: block;-ms-interpolation-mode: bicubic;height: auto;margin-bottom: 0;width: auto !important;max-width: 100% !important;margin: 0 auto;" width="550"/></a></td>
